import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const rootDir = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..");
const promptBankPath = path.join(rootDir, "ios/AiMuse/AiMuse/Resources/prompt_bank.json");
const outputDir = path.join(rootDir, "ios/AiMuse/AiMuse/Resources/seed_music");
const manifestPath = path.join(outputDir, "manifest.json");
const endpoint = process.env.MINIMAX_BASE_URL || "https://api.minimax.io/v1/music_generation";
const model = process.env.MINIMAX_MODEL || "music-2.6";
const targetCount = Number.parseInt(process.env.IOS_PROMPTBANK_TARGET_COUNT || "10", 10);
const batchPrefix = process.env.IOS_MUSIC_BATCH_PREFIX || "ios-promptbank";
const requestedIndex = Number.parseInt(process.argv[2] || "1", 10);

function readProperties(filePath) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) return {};
  return Object.fromEntries(
    fs.readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")
      .split(/\r?\n/)
      .map((line) => line.trim())
      .filter((line) => line && !line.startsWith("#") && line.includes("="))
      .map((line) => {
        const index = line.indexOf("=");
        return [line.slice(0, index).trim(), line.slice(index + 1).trim()];
      }),
  );
}

function apiKey() {
  return (
    process.env.MINIMAX_API_KEY?.trim() ||
    readProperties(path.join(rootDir, ".env.local")).MINIMAX_API_KEY ||
    readProperties(path.join(rootDir, "android/secrets.properties")).MINIMAX_API_KEY ||
    (() => { throw new Error("MINIMAX_API_KEY is missing."); })()
  );
}

function slug(value) {
  return String(value || "instrumental")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
    .slice(0, 54) || "instrumental";
}

function cleanPrompt(prompt) {
  return String(prompt || "")

    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();
}

      is_instrumental: true,
      output_format: "url",

  const baseResp = json.base_resp || {};

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const rootDir = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..");
const promptBankPath = path.join(rootDir, "ios/AiMuse/AiMuse/Resources/prompt_bank.json");
const outputDir = path.join(rootDir, "ios/AiMuse/AiMuse/Resources/seed_music");
const manifestPath = path.join(outputDir, "manifest.json");
const endpoint = process.env.MINIMAX_BASE_URL || "https://api.minimax.io/v1/music_generation";
const model = process.env.MINIMAX_MODEL || "music-2.6";
const targetCount = Number.parseInt(process.argv[2] || "50", 10);
const batchPrefix = process.env.IOS_MUSIC_BATCH_PREFIX || "ios-random-instrumental";
const generationTimeoutMs = Number.parseInt(process.env.MINIMAX_GENERATION_TIMEOUT_MS || "300000", 10);
const downloadTimeoutMs = Number.parseInt(process.env.MINIMAX_DOWNLOAD_TIMEOUT_MS || "180000", 10);

function readProperties(filePath) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) return {};
  return Object.fromEntries(
    fs
      .readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")
      .split(/\r?\n/)
      .map((line) => line.trim())
      .filter((line) => line && !line.startsWith("#") && line.includes("="))
      .map((line) => {
        const index = line.indexOf("=");
        return [line.slice(0, index).trim(), line.slice(index + 1).trim()];
      }),
  );
}

function apiKey() {
  return (
    process.env.MINIMAX_API_KEY?.trim() ||
    readProperties(path.join(rootDir, ".env.local")).MINIMAX_API_KEY ||
    readProperties(path.join(rootDir, "android/secrets.properties")).MINIMAX_API_KEY ||
    (() => {
      throw new Error("MINIMAX_API_KEY is missing.");
    })()
  );
}

function slug(value) {
  return String(value || "instrumental")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
    .slice(0, 54) || "instrumental";
}

function readManifest() {
  if (!fs.existsSync(manifestPath)) return [];
  <truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1126" />,
  };
}

async function main() {
  fs.mkdirSync(outputDir, { recursive: true });
  const promptBank = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(promptBankPath, "utf8"));
  const manifest = readManifest();
  const existingPrompts = new Set(manifest.map((entry) => String(entry.prompt || "").replace(/\b30 seconds?\b,?\s*/gi, "").trim()));
  const candidates = promptBank
    .filter((item) => item.mode !== "Song")
    .filter((item) => item.prompt && !existingPrompts.has(String(item.prompt).replace(/\b30 seconds?\b,?\s*/gi, "").trim()));

  const seed = Number.parseInt(process.env.IOS_RANDOM_SEED || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10).replaceAll("-", ""), 10);
  const queue = shuffled(candidates, seed).slice(0, targetCount);
  if (queue.length < targetCount) {
    throw new Error(`Only ${queue.length} unused instrumental prompts available.`);
  }

  let generated = 0;
  let failed = 0;
  for (const [offset, item] of queue.entries()) {
    const index = offset + 1;
    const fileName = fileNameFor(item, index);
    const latestManifest = readManifest();
    const alreadyDone = latestManifest.some((entry) => path.basename(entry.file || "") === fileName)
      && fs.existsSync(path.join(outputDir, fileName));
    if (alreadyDone) {
      console.log(`[${index}/${targetCount}] skip existing: ${item.title}`);
      continue;
    }

    try {
      const entry = await generateWithRetry(item, index);
      writeManifest(
        readManifest()
          .filter((track) => path.basename(track.file || "") !== fileName)
          .concat(entry),
      );
      generated += 1;
    } catch (error) {
      failed += 1;
      console.error(`[${index}/${targetCount}] failed permanently: ${error.message}`);
      if (/daily usage limit reached|insufficient|quota/i.test(error.message)) break;
    }
  }

  console.log(JSON.stringify({ generated, failed, ...summarize(readManifest()) }, null, 2));
}

main().catch((error) => {
  console.error(error.message);
  process.exit(1);
});

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const rootDir = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..");
const promptBankPath = path.join(rootDir, "ios/AiMuse/AiMuse/Resources/prompt_bank.json");
const outputDir = path.join(rootDir, "ios/AiMuse/AiMuse/Resources/seed_music");
const manifestPath = path.join(outputDir, "manifest.json");
const endpoint = process.env.MINIMAX_BASE_URL || "https://api.minimax.io/v1/music_generation";
const model = process.env.MINIMAX_MODEL || "music-2.6";
const targetCount = Number.parseInt(process.env.IOS_PROMPTBANK_TARGET_COUNT || "10", 10);
const batchPrefix = process.env.IOS_MUSIC_BATCH_PREFIX || "ios-promptbank";
const requestedIndex = Number.parseInt(process.argv[2] || "1", 10);

function readProperties(filePath) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) return {};
  return Object.fromEntries(
    fs.readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")
      .split(/\r?\n/)

  const json = JSON.parse(raw);
  const baseResp = json.base_resp || {};
  if (baseResp.status_code !== 0) throw new Error(baseResp.status_msg || "music generation failed");

  const audioUrl = json.data?.audio;
  if (!audioUrl || !/^https?:\/\//.test(audioUrl)) throw new Error("music generation returned no audio url");
  const audioResponse = await fetchWithRetry(audioUrl, {}, 3);
  if (!audioResponse.ok) throw new Error(`download failed ${audioResponse.status}: ${await audioResponse.text()}`);
  const bytes = Buffer.from(await audioResponse.arrayBuffer());
  fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, bytes);

  const entry = {
    title: item.title,
    mode: "Instrumental",
    prompt,
    file: `seed_music/${fileName}`,
    bytes: bytes.length,
    durationMs: json.extra_info?.music_duration ?? null,
    source: "iOS prompt bank single",
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  };
  writeManifest(readManifest().filter((track) => path.basename(track.file || "") !== fileName).concat(entry));
  console.log(`[${index}] done ${(bytes.length / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)} MB: ${item.title}`);
  return entry;
}

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const rootDir = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..");
const promptBankPath = path.join(rootDir, "ios/AiMuse/AiMuse/Resources/prompt_bank.json");
const outputDir = path.join(rootDir, "ios/AiMuse/AiMuse/Resources/seed_music");
const manifestPath = path.join(outputDir, "manifest.json");
const endpoint = process.env.MINIMAX_BASE_URL || "https://api.minimax.io/v1/music_generation";
const model = process.env.MINIMAX_MODEL || "music-2.6";
const targetCount = Number.parseInt(process.argv[2] || "50", 10);
const batchPrefix = process.env.IOS_MUSIC_BATCH_PREFIX || "ios-random-instrumental";
const generationTimeoutMs = Number.parseInt(process.env.MINIMAX_GENERATION_TIMEOUT_MS || "300000", 10);
const downloadTimeoutMs = Number.parseInt(process.env.MINIMAX_DOWNLOAD_TIMEOUT_MS || "180000", 10);

function readProperties(filePath) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) return {};
  return Object.fromEntries(
    fs
      .readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")
      .split(/\r?\n/)
      .map((line) => line.trim())
      .filter((line) => line && !line.startsWith("#") && line.includes("="))
      .map((line) => {
        const index = line.indexOf("=");
        return [line.slice(0, index).trim(), line.slice(index + 1).trim()];
      }),
  );
}

function apiKey() {
  return (
    process.env.MINIMAX_API_KEY?.trim() ||
    readProperties(path.join(rootDir, ".env.local")).MINIMAX_API_KEY ||
    readProperties(path.join(rootDir, "android/secrets.properties")).MINIMAX_API_KEY ||
    (() => {
      throw new Error("MINIMAX_API_KEY is missing.");
    })()
  );
}

function slug(value) {
  return String(value || "instrumental")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
    .slice(0, 54) || "instrumental";
}

function readManifest() {
  if (!fs.existsSync(manifestPath)) return [];
  <truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1126" />,
  };
}

async function main() {
  fs.mkdirSync(outputDir, { recursive: true });
  const promptBank = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(promptBankPath, "utf8"));
  const manifest = readManifest();
  const existingPrompts = new Set(manifest.map((entry) => String(entry.prompt || "").replace(/\b30 seconds?\b,?\s*/gi, "").trim()));
  const candidates = promptBank
    .filter((item) => item.mode !== "Song")
    .filter((item) => item.prompt && !existingPrompts.has(String(item.prompt).replace(/\b30 seconds?\b,?\s*/gi, "").trim()));

  const seed = Number.parseInt(process.env.IOS_RANDOM_SEED || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10).replaceAll("-", ""), 10);
  const queue = shuffled(candidates, seed).slice(0, targetCount);
  if (queue.length < targetCount) {
    throw new Error(`Only ${queue.length} unused instrumental prompts available.`);
  }

  let generated = 0;
  let failed = 0;
  for (const [offset, item] of queue.entries()) {
    const index = offset + 1;
    const fileName = fileNameFor(item, index);
    const latestManifest = readManifest();
    const alreadyDone = latestManifest.some((entry) => path.basename(entry.file || "") === fileName)
      && fs.existsSync(path.join(outputDir, fileName));
    if (alreadyDone) {
      console.log(`[${index}/${targetCount}] skip existing: ${item.title}`);
      continue;
    }

    try {
      const entry = await generateWithRetry(item, index);
      writeManifest(
        readManifest()
          .filter((track) => path.basename(track.file || "") !== fileName)
          .concat(entry),
      );
      generated += 1;
    } catch (error) {
      failed += 1;
      console.error(`[${index}/${targetCount}] failed permanently: ${error.message}`);
      if (/daily usage limit reached|insufficient|quota/i.test(error.message)) break;
    }
  }

  console.log(JSON.stringify({ generated, failed, ...summarize(readManifest()) }, null, 2));
}

main().catch((error) => {
  console.error(error.message);
  process.exit(1);
});

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const rootDir = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..");
const promptBankPath = path.join(rootDir, "ios/AiMuse/AiMuse/Resources/prompt_bank.json");
const outputDir = path.join(rootDir, "ios/AiMuse/AiMuse/Resources/seed_music");

  if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) return {};

    fs.readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")
      .split(/\r?\n/)
      .map((line) => line.trim())
      .filter((line) => line && !line.startsWith("#") && line.includes("="))
      .map((line) => {
        const index = line.indexOf("=");
        return [line.slice(0, index).trim(), line.slice(index + 1).trim()];
      }),
  );
}

    readProperties(path.join(rootDir, "android/secrets.properties")).MINIMAX_API_KEY ||
    (() => { throw new Error("MINIMAX_API_KEY is missing."); })()
  );
}

function slug(value) {

}

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const rootDir = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..");
const promptBankPath = path.join(rootDir, "ios/AiMuse/AiMuse/Resources/prompt_bank.json");
const outputDir = path.join(rootDir, "ios/AiMuse/AiMuse/Resources/seed_music");
const manifestPath = path.join(outputDir, "manifest.json");
const endpoint = process.env.MINIMAX_BASE_URL || "https://api.minimax.io/v1/music_generation";
const model = process.env.MINIMAX_MODEL || "music-2.6";
const targetCount = Number.parseInt(process.argv[2] || "50", 10);
const batchPrefix = process.env.IOS_MUSIC_BATCH_PREFIX || "ios-random-instrumental";
const generationTimeoutMs = Number.parseInt(process.env.MINIMAX_GENERATION_TIMEOUT_MS || "300000", 10);
const downloadTimeoutMs = Number.parseInt(process.env.MINIMAX_DOWNLOAD_TIMEOUT_MS || "180000", 10);

function readProperties(filePath) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) return {};
  return Object.fromEntries(
    fs
      .readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")
      .split(/\r?\n/)
      .map((line) => line.trim())
      .filter((line) => line && !line.startsWith("#") && line.includes("="))
      .map((line) => {
        const index = line.indexOf("=");
        return [line.slice(0, index).trim(), line.slice(index + 1).trim()];
      }),
  );
}

function apiKey() {
  return (
    process.env.MINIMAX_API_KEY?.trim() ||
    readProperties(path.join(rootDir, ".env.local")).MINIMAX_API_KEY ||
    readProperties(path.join(rootDir, "android/secrets.properties")).MINIMAX_API_KEY ||
    (() => {
      throw new Error("MINIMAX_API_KEY is missing.");
    })()
  );
}

function slug(value) {
  return String(value || "instrumental")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
    .slice(0, 54) || "instrumental";
}

function readManifest() {
  if (!fs.existsSync(manifestPath)) return [];
  <truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1126" />,
  };
}

async function main() {
  fs.mkdirSync(outputDir, { recursive: true });
  const promptBank = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(promptBankPath, "utf8"));
  const manifest = readManifest();
  const existingPrompts = new Set(manifest.map((entry) => String(entry.prompt || "").replace(/\b30 seconds?\b,?\s*/gi, "").trim()));
  const candidates = promptBank
    .filter((item) => item.mode !== "Song")
    .filter((item) => item.prompt && !existingPrompts.has(String(item.prompt).replace(/\b30 seconds?\b,?\s*/gi, "").trim()));

  const seed = Number.parseInt(process.env.IOS_RANDOM_SEED || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10).replaceAll("-", ""), 10);
  const queue = shuffled(candidates, seed).slice(0, targetCount);
  if (queue.length < targetCount) {
    throw new Error(`Only ${queue.length} unused instrumental prompts available.`);
  }

  let generated = 0;
  let failed = 0;
  for (const [offset, item] of queue.entries()) {
    const index = offset + 1;
    const fileName = fileNameFor(item, index);
    const latestManifest = readManifest();
    const alreadyDone = latestManifest.some((entry) => path.basename(entry.file || "") === fileName)
      && fs.existsSync(path.join(outputDir, fileName));
    if (alreadyDone) {
      console.log(`[${index}/${targetCount}] skip existing: ${item.title}`);
      continue;
    }

    try {
      const entry = await generateWithRetry(item, index);
      writeManifest(
        readManifest()
          .filter((track) => path.basename(track.file || "") !== fileName)
          .concat(entry),
      );
      generated += 1;
    } catch (error) {
      failed += 1;
      console.error(`[${index}/${targetCount}] failed permanently: ${error.message}`);
      if (/daily usage limit reached|insufficient|quota/i.test(error.message)) break;
    }
  }

  console.log(JSON.stringify({ generated, failed, ...summarize(readManifest()) }, null, 2));
}

main().catch((error) => {
  console.error(error.message);
  process.exit(1);
});

async function fetchWithRetry(url, options, attempts = 3, timeoutMs = 300000) {
  let lastError;
  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= attempts; attempt += 1) {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(new Error(`request timed out after ${timeoutMs}ms`)), timeoutMs);
    try {
      log("fetch:attempt", { url: safeUrl(url), attempt, timeoutMs });
      const response = await fetch(url, { ...options, signal: controller.signal });
      if (response.status < 500 || attempt === attempts) return response;
      lastError = new Error(`server returned ${response.status}: ${await response.text()}`);
      log("fetch:retryable-status", { url: safeUrl(url), attempt, status: response.status });
    } catch (error) {
      lastError = error;
      log("fetch:failed", {
        url: safeUrl(url),
        attempt,
        name: error.name,
        code: error.code || error.cause?.code,
        cause: error.cause?.message,
        message: error.message,
      });
      if (attempt === attempts) throw error;
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timeout);
    }
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, attempt * 3000));
  }
  throw lastError;
}

function safeUrl(value) {
  try {
    const url = new URL(value);
    return `${url.protocol}//${url.host}${url.pathname}`;
  } catch {
    return "unknown";
  }
}

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const rootDir = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..");
const promptBankPath = path.join(rootDir, "ios/AiMuse/AiMuse/Resources/prompt_bank.json");
const outputDir = path.join(rootDir, "ios/AiMuse/AiMuse/Resources/seed_music");
const manifestPath = path.join(outputDir, "manifest.json");
const endpoint = process.env.MINIMAX_BASE_URL || "https://api.minimax.io/v1/music_generation";
const model = process.env.MINIMAX_MODEL || "music-2.6";
const targetCount = Number.parseInt(process.env.IOS_PROMPTBANK_TARGET_COUNT || "10", 10);
const batchPrefix = process.env.IOS_MUSIC_BATCH_PREFIX || "ios-promptbank";
const requestedIndex = Number.parseInt(process.argv[2] || "1", 10);

function readProperties(filePath) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) return {};
  return Object.fromEntries(
    fs.readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")
      .split(/\r?\n/)
      .map((line) => line.trim())
      .filter((line) => line && !line.startsWith("#") && line.includes("="))
      .map((line) => {
        const index = line.indexOf("=");
        return [line.slice(0, index).trim(), line.slice(index + 1).trim()];
      }),
  );
}

function apiKey() {
  return (
    process.env.MINIMAX_API_KEY?.trim() ||
    readProperties(path.join(rootDir, ".env.local")).MINIMAX_API_KEY ||
    readProperties(path.join(rootDir, "android/secrets.properties")).MINIMAX_API_KEY ||
    (() => { throw new Error("MINIMAX_API_KEY is missing."); })()
  );
}

function slug(value) {
  return String(value || "instrumental")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
    .slice(0, 54) || "instrumental";
}

function cleanPrompt(prompt) {
  return String(prompt || "")
    .replace(/\b30 seconds?\b,?\s*/gi, "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();
}

      output_format: "url",

  const json = JSON.parse(raw);
  const baseResp = json.base_resp || {};
  if (baseResp.status_code !== 0) throw new Error(baseResp.status_msg || "music generation failed");

  const audioUrl = json.data?.audio;
  if (!audioUrl || !/^https?:\/\//.test(audioUrl)) throw new Error("music generation returned no audio url");

  const bytes = Buffer.from(await audioResponse.arrayBuffer());

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const rootDir = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..");
const promptBankPath = path.join(rootDir, "ios/AiMuse/AiMuse/Resources/prompt_bank.json");
const outputDir = path.join(rootDir, "ios/AiMuse/AiMuse/Resources/seed_music");
const manifestPath = path.join(outputDir, "manifest.json");
const endpoint = process.env.MINIMAX_BASE_URL || "https://api.minimax.io/v1/music_generation";
const model = process.env.MINIMAX_MODEL || "music-2.6";
const targetCount = Number.parseInt(process.argv[2] || "50", 10);
const batchPrefix = process.env.IOS_MUSIC_BATCH_PREFIX || "ios-random-instrumental";
const generationTimeoutMs = Number.parseInt(process.env.MINIMAX_GENERATION_TIMEOUT_MS || "300000", 10);
const downloadTimeoutMs = Number.parseInt(process.env.MINIMAX_DOWNLOAD_TIMEOUT_MS || "180000", 10);

function readProperties(filePath) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) return {};
  return Object.fromEntries(
    fs
      .readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")
      .split(/\r?\n/)
      .map((line) => line.trim())
      .filter((line) => line && !line.startsWith("#") && line.includes("="))
      .map((line) => {
        const index = line.indexOf("=");
        return [line.slice(0, index).trim(), line.slice(index + 1).trim()];
      }),
  );
}

function apiKey() {
  return (
    process.env.MINIMAX_API_KEY?.trim() ||
    readProperties(path.join(rootDir, ".env.local")).MINIMAX_API_KEY ||
    readProperties(path.join(rootDir, "android/secrets.properties")).MINIMAX_API_KEY ||
    (() => {
      throw new Error("MINIMAX_API_KEY is missing.");
    })()
  );
}

function slug(value) {
  return String(value || "instrumental")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
    .slice(0, 54) || "instrumental";
}

function readManifest() {
  if (!fs.existsSync(manifestPath)) return [];
  <truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1126" />,
  };
}

async function main() {
  fs.mkdirSync(outputDir, { recursive: true });
  const promptBank = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(promptBankPath, "utf8"));
  const manifest = readManifest();
  const existingPrompts = new Set(manifest.map((entry) => String(entry.prompt || "").replace(/\b30 seconds?\b,?\s*/gi, "").trim()));
  const candidates = promptBank
    .filter((item) => item.mode !== "Song")
    .filter((item) => item.prompt && !existingPrompts.has(String(item.prompt).replace(/\b30 seconds?\b,?\s*/gi, "").trim()));

  const seed = Number.parseInt(process.env.IOS_RANDOM_SEED || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10).replaceAll("-", ""), 10);
  const queue = shuffled(candidates, seed).slice(0, targetCount);
  if (queue.length < targetCount) {
    throw new Error(`Only ${queue.length} unused instrumental prompts available.`);
  }

  let generated = 0;
  let failed = 0;
  for (const [offset, item] of queue.entries()) {
    const index = offset + 1;
    const fileName = fileNameFor(item, index);
    const latestManifest = readManifest();
    const alreadyDone = latestManifest.some((entry) => path.basename(entry.file || "") === fileName)
      && fs.existsSync(path.join(outputDir, fileName));
    if (alreadyDone) {
      console.log(`[${index}/${targetCount}] skip existing: ${item.title}`);
      continue;
    }

    try {
      const entry = await generateWithRetry(item, index);
      writeManifest(
        readManifest()
          .filter((track) => path.basename(track.file || "") !== fileName)
          .concat(entry),
      );
      generated += 1;
    } catch (error) {
      failed += 1;
      console.error(`[${index}/${targetCount}] failed permanently: ${error.message}`);
      if (/daily usage limit reached|insufficient|quota/i.test(error.message)) break;
    }
  }

  console.log(JSON.stringify({ generated, failed, ...summarize(readManifest()) }, null, 2));
}

main().catch((error) => {
  console.error(error.message);
  process.exit(1);
});

async function fetchWithRetry(url, options, attempts = 3, timeoutMs = 300000) {
  let lastError;
  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= attempts; attempt += 1) {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(new Error(`request timed out after ${timeoutMs}ms`)), timeoutMs);
    try {
      log("fetch:attempt", { url: safeUrl(url), attempt, timeoutMs });
      const response = await fetch(url, { ...options, signal: controller.signal });
      if (response.status < 500 || attempt === attempts) return response;
      lastError = new Error(`server returned ${response.status}: ${await response.text()}`);
      log("fetch:retryable-status", { url: safeUrl(url), attempt, status: response.status });
    } catch (error) {
      lastError = error;
      log("fetch:failed", {
        url: safeUrl(url),
        attempt,
        name: error.name,
        code: error.code || error.cause?.code,
        cause: error.cause?.message,
        message: error.message,
      });
      if (attempt === attempts) throw error;
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timeout);
    }
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, attempt * 3000));
  }
  throw lastError;
}

function safeUrl(value) {
  try {
    const url = new URL(value);
    return `${url.protocol}//${url.host}${url.pathname}`;
  } catch {
    return "unknown";
  }
}

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const rootDir = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..");
const promptBankPath = path.join(rootDir, "ios/AiMuse/AiMuse/Resources/prompt_bank.json");
const outputDir = path.join(rootDir, "ios/AiMuse/AiMuse/Resources/seed_music");
const manifestPath = path.join(outputDir, "manifest.json");
const endpoint = process.env.MINIMAX_BASE_URL || "https://api.minimax.io/v1/music_generation";
const model = process.env.MINIMAX_MODEL || "music-2.6";
const targetCount = Number.parseInt(process.env.IOS_PROMPTBANK_TARGET_COUNT || "10", 10);
const batchPrefix = process.env.IOS_MUSIC_BATCH_PREFIX || "ios-promptbank";
const requestedIndex = Number.parseInt(process.argv[2] || "1", 10);

function readProperties(filePath) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) return {};
  return Object.fromEntries(
    fs.readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")
      .split(/\r?\n/)
      .map((line) => line.trim())
      .filter((line) => line && !line.startsWith("#") && line.includes("="))
      .map((line) => {
        const index = line.indexOf("=");
        return [line.slice(0, index).trim(), line.slice(index + 1).trim()];
      }),
  );
}

function apiKey() {
  return (
    process.env.MINIMAX_API_KEY?.trim() ||
    readProperties(path.join(rootDir, ".env.local")).MINIMAX_API_KEY ||
    readProperties(path.join(rootDir, "android/secrets.properties")).MINIMAX_API_KEY ||
    (() => { throw new Error("MINIMAX_API_KEY is missing."); })()
  );
}

function slug(value) {
  return String(value || "instrumental")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
    .slice(0, 54) || "instrumental";
}

function cleanPrompt(prompt) {
  return String(prompt || "")
    .replace(/\b30 seconds?\b,?\s*/gi, "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();
}

    body: JSON.stringify({

  const json = JSON.parse(raw);
  const baseResp = json.base_resp || {};

  const audioUrl = json.data?.audio;
  if (!audioUrl || !/^https?:\/\//.test(audioUrl)) throw new Error("music generation returned no audio url");

const promptBankPath = path.join(rootDir, "ios/AiMuse/AiMuse/Resources/prompt_bank.json");
const outputDir = path.join(rootDir, "ios/AiMuse/AiMuse/Resources/seed_music");
const manifestPath = path.join(outputDir, "manifest.json");
const endpoint = process.env.MINIMAX_BASE_URL || "https://api.minimax.io/v1/music_generation";
const model = process.env.MINIMAX_MODEL || "music-2.6";
const targetCount = Number.parseInt(process.argv[2] || "50", 10);
const batchPrefix = process.env.IOS_MUSIC_BATCH_PREFIX || "ios-random-instrumental";
const generationTimeoutMs = Number.parseInt(process.env.MINIMAX_GENERATION_TIMEOUT_MS || "300000", 10);
const downloadTimeoutMs = Number.parseInt(process.env.MINIMAX_DOWNLOAD_TIMEOUT_MS || "180000", 10);

function readProperties(filePath) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) return {};
  return Object.fromEntries(
    fs
      .readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")
      .split(/\r?\n/)
      .map((line) => line.trim())
      .filter((line) => line && !line.startsWith("#") && line.includes("="))
      .map((line) => {
        const index = line.indexOf("=");
        return [line.slice(0, index).trim(), line.slice(index + 1).trim()];
      }),
  );
}

function apiKey() {
  return (
    process.env.MINIMAX_API_KEY?.trim() ||
    readProperties(path.join(rootDir, ".env.local")).MINIMAX_API_KEY ||
    readProperties(path.join(rootDir, "android/secrets.properties")).MINIMAX_API_KEY ||
    (() => {
      throw new Error("MINIMAX_API_KEY is missing.");
    })()
  );
}

function slug(value) {
  return String(value || "instrumental")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
    .slice(0, 54) || "instrumental";
}

function readManifest() {
  if (!fs.existsSync(manifestPath)) return [];
  <truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1126" />,
  };
}

async function main() {
  fs.mkdirSync(outputDir, { recursive: true });
  const promptBank = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(promptBankPath, "utf8"));
  const manifest = readManifest();
  const existingPrompts = new Set(manifest.map((entry) => String(entry.prompt || "").replace(/\b30 seconds?\b,?\s*/gi, "").trim()));
  const candidates = promptBank
    .filter((item) => item.mode !== "Song")
    .filter((item) => item.prompt && !existingPrompts.has(String(item.prompt).replace(/\b30 seconds?\b,?\s*/gi, "").trim()));

  const seed = Number.parseInt(process.env.IOS_RANDOM_SEED || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10).replaceAll("-", ""), 10);
  const queue = shuffled(candidates, seed).slice(0, targetCount);
  if (queue.length < targetCount) {
    throw new Error(`Only ${queue.length} unused instrumental prompts available.`);
  }

  let generated = 0;
  let failed = 0;
  for (const [offset, item] of queue.entries()) {
    const index = offset + 1;
    const fileName = fileNameFor(item, index);
    const latestManifest = readManifest();
    const alreadyDone = latestManifest.some((entry) => path.basename(entry.file || "") === fileName)
      && fs.existsSync(path.join(outputDir, fileName));
    if (alreadyDone) {
      console.log(`[${index}/${targetCount}] skip existing: ${item.title}`);
      continue;
    }

    try {
      const entry = await generateWithRetry(item, index);
      writeManifest(
        readManifest()
          .filter((track) => path.basename(track.file || "") !== fileName)
          .concat(entry),
      );
      generated += 1;
    } catch (error) {
      failed += 1;
      console.error(`[${index}/${targetCount}] failed permanently: ${error.message}`);
      if (/daily usage limit reached|insufficient|quota/i.test(error.message)) break;
    }
  }

  console.log(JSON.stringify({ generated, failed, ...summarize(readManifest()) }, null, 2));
}

main().catch((error) => {
  console.error(error.message);
  process.exit(1);
});

async function fetchWithRetry(url, options, attempts = 3, timeoutMs = 300000) {
  let lastError;
  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= attempts; attempt += 1) {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(new Error(`request timed out after ${timeoutMs}ms`)), timeoutMs);
    try {
      log("fetch:attempt", { url: safeUrl(url), attempt, timeoutMs });
      const response = await fetch(url, { ...options, signal: controller.signal });
      if (response.status < 500 || attempt === attempts) return response;
      lastError = new Error(`server returned ${response.status}: ${await response.text()}`);
      log("fetch:retryable-status", { url: safeUrl(url), attempt, status: response.status });
    } catch (error) {
      lastError = error;
      log("fetch:failed", {
        url: safeUrl(url),
        attempt,
        name: error.name,
        code: error.code || error.cause?.code,
        cause: error.cause?.message,
        message: error.message,
      });
      if (attempt === attempts) throw error;
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timeout);
    }
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, attempt * 3000));
  }
  throw lastError;
}

function safeUrl(value) {
  try {
    const url = new URL(value);
    return `${url.protocol}//${url.host}${url.pathname}`;
  } catch {
    return "unknown";
  }
}

}

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const rootDir = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..");
const promptBankPath = path.join(rootDir, "ios/AiMuse/AiMuse/Resources/prompt_bank.json");
const outputDir = path.join(rootDir, "ios/AiMuse/AiMuse/Resources/seed_music");
const manifestPath = path.join(outputDir, "manifest.json");
const endpoint = process.env.MINIMAX_BASE_URL || "https://api.minimax.io/v1/music_generation";
const model = process.env.MINIMAX_MODEL || "music-2.6";
const targetCount = Number.parseInt(process.argv[2] || "50", 10);
const batchPrefix = process.env.IOS_MUSIC_BATCH_PREFIX || "ios-random-instrumental";
const generationTimeoutMs = Number.parseInt(process.env.MINIMAX_GENERATION_TIMEOUT_MS || "300000", 10);
const downloadTimeoutMs = Number.parseInt(process.env.MINIMAX_DOWNLOAD_TIMEOUT_MS || "180000", 10);

function readProperties(filePath) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) return {};
  return Object.fromEntries(
    fs
      .readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")
      .split(/\r?\n/)
      .map((line) => line.trim())
      .filter((line) => line && !line.startsWith("#") && line.includes("="))
      .map((line) => {
        const index = line.indexOf("=");
        return [line.slice(0, index).trim(), line.slice(index + 1).trim()];
      }),
  );
}

function apiKey() {
  return (
    process.env.MINIMAX_API_KEY?.trim() ||
    readProperties(path.join(rootDir, ".env.local")).MINIMAX_API_KEY ||
    readProperties(path.join(rootDir, "android/secrets.properties")).MINIMAX_API_KEY ||
    (() => {
      throw new Error("MINIMAX_API_KEY is missing.");
    })()
  );
}

function slug(value) {
  return String(value || "instrumental")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
    .slice(0, 54) || "instrumental";
}

function readManifest() {
  if (!fs.existsSync(manifestPath)) return [];
  <truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1126" />,
  };
}

async function main() {
  fs.mkdirSync(outputDir, { recursive: true });
  const promptBank = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(promptBankPath, "utf8"));
  const manifest = readManifest();
  const existingPrompts = new Set(manifest.map((entry) => String(entry.prompt || "").replace(/\b30 seconds?\b,?\s*/gi, "").trim()));
  const candidates = promptBank
    .filter((item) => item.mode !== "Song")
    .filter((item) => item.prompt && !existingPrompts.has(String(item.prompt).replace(/\b30 seconds?\b,?\s*/gi, "").trim()));

  const seed = Number.parseInt(process.env.IOS_RANDOM_SEED || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10).replaceAll("-", ""), 10);
  const queue = shuffled(candidates, seed).slice(0, targetCount);
  if (queue.length < targetCount) {
    throw new Error(`Only ${queue.length} unused instrumental prompts available.`);
  }

  let generated = 0;
  let failed = 0;
  for (const [offset, item] of queue.entries()) {
    const index = offset + 1;
    const fileName = fileNameFor(item, index);
    const latestManifest = readManifest();
    const alreadyDone = latestManifest.some((entry) => path.basename(entry.file || "") === fileName)
      && fs.existsSync(path.join(outputDir, fileName));
    if (alreadyDone) {
      console.log(`[${index}/${targetCount}] skip existing: ${item.title}`);
      continue;
    }

    try {
      const entry = await generateWithRetry(item, index);
      writeManifest(
        readManifest()
          .filter((track) => path.basename(track.file || "") !== fileName)
          .concat(entry),
      );
      generated += 1;
    } catch (error) {
      failed += 1;
      console.error(`[${index}/${targetCount}] failed permanently: ${error.message}`);
      if (/daily usage limit reached|insufficient|quota/i.test(error.message)) break;
    }
  }

  console.log(JSON.stringify({ generated, failed, ...summarize(readManifest()) }, null, 2));
}

main().catch((error) => {
  console.error(error.message);
  process.exit(1);
});

async function fetchWithRetry(url, options, attempts = 3, timeoutMs = 300000) {
  let lastError;
  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= attempts; attempt += 1) {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(new Error(`request timed out after ${timeoutMs}ms`)), timeoutMs);
    try {
      log("fetch:attempt", { url: safeUrl(url), attempt, timeoutMs });
      const response = await fetch(url, { ...options, signal: controller.signal });
      if (response.status < 500 || attempt === attempts) return response;
      lastError = new Error(`server returned ${response.status}: ${await response.text()}`);
      log("fetch:retryable-status", { url: safeUrl(url), attempt, status: response.status });
    } catch (error) {
      lastError = error;
      log("fetch:failed", {
        url: safeUrl(url),
        attempt,
        name: error.name,
        code: error.code || error.cause?.code,
        cause: error.cause?.message,
        message: error.message,
      });
      if (attempt === attempts) throw error;
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timeout);
    }
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, attempt * 3000));
  }
  throw lastError;
}

function safeUrl(value) {
  try {
    const url = new URL(value);
    return `${url.protocol}//${url.host}${url.pathname}`;
  } catch {
    return "unknown";
  }
}

async function fetchWithRetry(url, options, attempts = 3, timeoutMs = 300000) {
  let lastError;
  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= attempts; attempt += 1) {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(new Error(`request timed out after ${timeoutMs}ms`)), timeoutMs);
    try {
      log("fetch:attempt", { url: safeUrl(url), attempt, timeoutMs });
      const response = await fetch(url, { ...options, signal: controller.signal });
      if (response.status < 500 || attempt === attempts) return response;
      lastError = new Error(`server returned ${response.status}: ${await response.text()}`);
      log("fetch:retryable-status", { url: safeUrl(url), attempt, status: response.status });
    } catch (error) {
      lastError = error;
      log("fetch:failed", {
        url: safeUrl(url),
        attempt,
        name: error.name,
        code: error.code || error.cause?.code,
        cause: error.cause?.message,
        message: error.message,
      });
      if (attempt === attempts) throw error;
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timeout);
    }
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, attempt * 3000));
  }
  throw lastError;
}

function safeUrl(value) {
  try {
    const url = new URL(value);
    return `${url.protocol}//${url.host}${url.pathname}`;
  } catch {
    return "unknown";
  }
}

async function fetchWithRetry(url, options, attempts = 3, timeoutMs = 300000) {
  let lastError;
  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= attempts; attempt += 1) {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(new Error(`request timed out after ${timeoutMs}ms`)), timeoutMs);
    try {
      log("fetch:attempt", { url: safeUrl(url), attempt, timeoutMs });
      const response = await fetch(url, { ...options, signal: controller.signal });
      if (response.status < 500 || attempt === attempts) return response;
      lastError = new Error(`server returned ${response.status}: ${await response.text()}`);
      log("fetch:retryable-status", { url: safeUrl(url), attempt, status: response.status });
    } catch (error) {
      lastError = error;
      log("fetch:failed", {
        url: safeUrl(url),
        attempt,
        name: error.name,
        code: error.code || error.cause?.code,
        cause: error.cause?.message,
        message: error.message,
      });
      if (attempt === attempts) throw error;
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timeout);
    }
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, attempt * 3000));
  }
  throw lastError;
}

function safeUrl(value) {
  try {
    const url = new URL(value);
    return `${url.protocol}//${url.host}${url.pathname}`;
  } catch {
    return "unknown";
  }
}
